    text-to-image-free>其他 · Hermes · v1.3.0
    🎨 Text-to-Image Free
    


    A free AI image generation tool. User provides a text description, and the agent generates a corresponding image. No API key required, zero cost. Powered by Pollinations.ai free API.
    


    Usage
    


    User says: "generate an image: a Shiba Inu on a beach drinking coconut water"
    


    The agent performs the following workflow:
    


    Step 1: Extract Prompt
    


    Extract the image description from user input.
    


    Trigger keywords: "generate", "create an image", "draw", "make a picture"
    


    Step 2: Set Parameters
    


    | Parameter | Default | Description |
    

    |-----------|---------|-------------|
    

    | width | 1024 | Image width in px |
    

    | height | 768 | Image height in px |
    

    | model | sana | sana / flux / turbo / dreamshaper |
    

    | nologo | true | Remove watermark |
    

    | seed | random | Fixed integer for reproducible results |
    


    User can override:
    

    "Generate: cyberpunk Shanghai night view, width 1920 height 1080, use flux model"
"Generate another, use seed=42 for consistent style"

    


    Step 3: Call API
    


    Use exec to run curl against Pollinations.ai:
    


    PROMPT="<your text description>"
WIDTH=1024
HEIGHT=768
SEED=""  # optional: "42"

curl -s --max-time 180 \
  "https://image.pollinations.ai/prompt/$(python3 -c "import urllib.parse; print(urllib.parse.quote('$PROMPT'))")?width=$WIDTH&height=$HEIGHT&nologo=true${SEED:+&seed=$SEED}" \
  -o /tmp/generated_image.jpg

    


    Step 4: Return Result
    


    Return the generated image file to the user.
    


    Model Selection
    


    | Model | Features | Speed |
    

    |-------|----------|-------|
    

    | sana (default) | Latest efficient model, balanced style & detail | Fast |
    

    | flux | Higher quality, richer detail | Moderate |
    

    | turbo | Fastest generation, good for previews | Fastest |
    

    | dreamshaper | Artistic / dream-like style | Medium |
    


    > As of May 2026, Pollinations.ai default model has been switched to Sana. Older models like stable-diffusion are deprecated. Sana offers better speed-quality balance.
    


    Examples
    


    User: "Generate: cyberpunk Shanghai night, neon lights, flying cars"
→ Agent calls API → returns image ✓

User: "Generate: an orange cat wearing an astronaut helmet, cartoon style, use flux model"
→ Agent calls API → returns image ✓

    


    Notes
    


  • Free API has queue limits: one request at a time
    • 

  • Generation takes ~15-60 seconds (Sana is faster than predecessors)
    • 

  • English prompts yield best results
    • 

  • Use seed parameter (e.g., &seed=42) for reproducible results
    • 

  • Non-commercial use only (Pollinations.ai free tier limitation)
    • 


    ---
    


    🎨 文生图（免费）
    


    一个免费的AI图片生成工具。用户输入描述性文字，即可生成对应的图片。无需任何API Key，零成本使用。基于 Pollinations.ai 免费API。
    


    使用方法
    


    用户说："画一张图：一只柴犬在沙滩上喝椰子水"
    


    Agent 执行以下流程：
    


    第一步：提取提示词
    


    从用户输入中提取图片描述文本。
    


    关键词触发："画一张图"/"生成图片"/"画"/"create"/"generate"
    


    第二步：调整参数
    


    | 参数 | 默认值 | 说明 |
    

    |------|--------|------|
    

    | width | 1024 | 图片宽度 |
    

    | height | 768 | 图片高度 |
    

    | model | sana | sana / flux / turbo / dreamshaper |
    

    | 无水印 | 是 | 默认去除水印 |
    

    | seed | 随机 | 固定值可复现相同结果 |
    


    用户可指定参数：
    

    "画一张图：赛博朋克上海夜景，宽1920高1080，使用flux模型"
"再生成一次，用seed=42保持风格一致"

    


    第三步：调用API
    


    使用 exec 运行 curl 命令调用 Pollinations.ai：
    


    PROMPT="<图片描述文字>"
WIDTH=1024
HEIGHT=768
SEED=""  # 可选："42"

curl -s --max-time 180 \
  "https://image.pollinations.ai/prompt/$(python3 -c "import urllib.parse; print(urllib.parse.quote('$PROMPT'))")?width=$WIDTH&height=$HEIGHT&nologo=true${SEED:+&seed=$SEED}" \
  -o /tmp/generated_image.jpg

    


    第四步：返回结果
    


    将生成的图片文件返回给用户。
    


    模型选择
    


    | 模型 | 特点 | 速度 |
    

    |------|------|------|
    

    | sana（默认） | 最新高效模型，通用风格，细节出色 | 快 |
    

    | flux | 更高质量，细节丰富 | 稍慢 |
    

    | turbo | 极速生成，适合快速预览 | 最快 |
    

    | dreamshaper | 偏向艺术/梦幻风格 | 中等 |
    


    > 2026年5月更新：Pollinations.ai 已将默认模型切换为 Sana，前代模型（如stable-diffusion）已下线。Sana在速度和画质间取得更好平衡。
    


    示例
    


    用户：「画一张图：赛博朋克上海夜景，霓虹灯，飞行汽车」
→ Agent 调用API → 返回图片 ✓

用户：「画一张图：一只橘猫戴着宇航员头盔，卡通风格，使用flux模型」
→ Agent 调用API → 返回图片 ✓

    


    注意事项
    


  • 免费API有队列限制，一次只能处理一个请求
    • 

  • 生成时间约15-60秒（Sana模型较前代更快）
    • 

  • 建议使用英文提示词获得最佳效果，中文也支持
    • 

  • 支持 seed 参数（如 &seed=42）获得可复现结果
    • 

  • 仅限非商业用途（Pollinations.ai免费层限制）
    • trading-osUS swing-trading research: TV scans and ORH/RVOL alerts.其他 · Hermes · v1.0.0 · 68 個支援檔
    Hermes Trading OS — US swing-trading research system
    


    Class: long-only US swing-trading research, screening, watchlist/focus-list, intraday alerts, paper-trading, journal and meta review. Framework: the user's SOP — Jeff Sun's public workflow plus a 5-minute ORH / RVOL final trigger. The pasted SOP is the governing document; re-read it before acting if the project state below is stale.
    


    Topic depth lives in references/: measurement-discipline.md — every way a number here can be confidently WRONG (caches, replays, unmeasured vetoes, frozen thresholds, missing-as-zero, same-label/different-definition, join keys, controls, verdict margins, counterfactual input sets, entry/exit confound separation and who runs the comparison, tests writing to the live ledger). notion-kb-ingest.md — Notion ingest contract. adding-a-deterministic-job.md — the nine-piece checklist for shipping a new engine + config + tests + cron into this pipeline: the output namespace guard, dropping the partial bar, DATA_UNAVAILABLE never 0, idempotent daily history + --backfill, the wrapper's env isolation, registering the job in edge_book/job_descriptions.json, and what "green" means when a sibling tree is being edited concurrently. instrument-risk-calibration.md — traded vs scanned universe, stops from ATR% per instrument, the noise-floor method, base drawdown rates, what ATRX does not predict, leveraged-ETF decay, close-time scheduling from HKT, cost-in-R, the tight-stop-to-churn link. case-reporting.md — delivering a case: CJK chart glyph traps, the render-then-view layout loop, logging to Notion via the project's own token. Read these before reporting any expectancy, win rate, stop size, or replay result.
    


    When to Use
    

    Load this skill for any work on the user's US swing-trading research system — the TradingView scan pipeline, layer 1–5 modules, the daily_plan.py card, the setup_alerts.py 7-setup intraday engine, breadth, the Edge Book, the trade journal, and its cron jobs. Also load it to answer "點解個警報冇出／出錯", "點解冇訊號", "how much does this cost / how do I cut it", or anything about a ticker in his pool. Two rules come before everything else: re-read the SOP file before changing a strategy parameter, and never emit an entry signal (ELIGIBLE_FOR_MANUAL_REVIEW is the strongest output).
    


    Non-negotiables (highest priority, every run)
    

  • 數字完整性：任何比率嘅分母只計可量度嘅列；跑 audit 前先剔走未完成交易日。詳見 references/audit-integrity.md。
    • 

  • 量度單位必須同標的一致。 一套系統量到嘅勝率／賠率／期望值，唔可以餵入另一套系統嘅模型——唔同工具、唔同 R 單位、唔同出場規則，個數冇意義。引用任何跨系統數字之前，先答得出「呢個數係喺邊個工具、邊個 R 單位上面量出嚟」。詳見 references/measurement-integrity.md。
    • 

  • Research only. Never connect to brokers/exchanges/OMS, never place or assist semi-automatic orders, never request, store or touch broker credentials/2FA/API secrets.
    • 

  • Positive execution output may ONLY be: ELIGIBLE_FOR_MANUAL_REVIEW, REJECTED, WATCH_ONLY, DATA_UNAVAILABLE. Never emit "buy now / 買入 / 建倉" or equivalents.
    • 

  • Every number (price, volume, RVOL, ATR, earnings date, short float, breadth) must be traceable to source + timestamp + computation function. No guessing, imputation or LLM-sourced prices.
    • 

  • Missing, stale, misaligned or unverifiable data → DATA_UNAVAILABLE. Never treat it as a pass.
    • 

  • All strategy parameters live in versioned YAML config; core thresholds must not be hard-coded in logic (existing configs: config/breadth.yaml for Layer 4; Layer 3 params inline per EDGE-024).
    • 

  • Two-door authorization (adopted 2026-10-05). Classify BEFORE asking.
    • 

      - Two-way door — REVERSIBLE, small blast radius. Act first, report after. Do not ask, do not wait. Renaming a file, adding a comment/docstring/type, adding a test, writing under cache/scratch/, running any read-only script or analysis, running the test suite, adding a guard or assertion, creating a NEW module that nothing reads yet, re-running an existing no_agent job by hand. Rationale: the revert costs less than the round-trip, and a pending question stalls the whole pipeline
